#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}

+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);

+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,

codex
The CUDA build succeeds, and validation is passing on small matrices and tile boundaries. Comparisons show minor rounding differences from fused multiply-add instructions; I’ll disable contraction to match the original arithmetic more closely, then check larger cases and multi-GPU execution. Compute Sanitizer could not run because its injection library is missing.

-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+

+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1

+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;

+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {

+                } catch (...) {

+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;

+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {

+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;

+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
s=Path('matmul/matmul.cpp').read_text().replace('#include <cuda_runtime.h>','')
a=s.index('void checkCuda(')
b=s.index('// Simple validation:',a)

                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

'''+s[b:]

+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }

+    }

+        #pragma unroll

+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }

+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};

+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));

+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);

+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,

+    int deviceCount = 0;

+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);

+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,

+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {

+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1

+    if (workers == 1) {

+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;

+    } catch (...) {

+        throw;
     }

+    }

I found a working Compute Sanitizer installation; it reports no memory errors or shared-memory races in the tested cases. The CPU compiler also fuses some operations with its original optimization flags, so bitwise results can differ. -void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Small problems cannot amortize replication of B across multiple GPUs.
+    const int workers = N < 2048 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
+    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
+    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }
