-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {

-            double sum = 0.0;

-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
+    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {

+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {

+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();

+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};

+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}

+    int deviceCount = 0;

+    }
+    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
+    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1

codex
Full-matrix hashes match the original loop with contraction disabled at every tested size through 2049, including partial tiles. The 2049 case took about 2.1 seconds versus 24.2 seconds on the CPU. Multi-GPU setup costs outweighed the benefit at 4096, so I raised the threshold and am checking an 8193 case on one and four GPUs.

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
                              const double* __restrict__ B,
                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
    constexpr int tile = 64;
    constexpr int depth = 16;
    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
    double sums[4][4] = {};
    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;

    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
            const int ar = offset / depth;
            const int ak = offset % depth;
            const int bk = offset / tile;
            const int bc = offset % tile;
            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
        }
        __syncthreads();
        #pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
            double av[4], bv[4];
            #pragma unroll
            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
            }
            #pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
                #pragma unroll
                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
                }
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
            if (row < rows && col < N) {
                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
            }
        }
    }
}

struct DeviceBuffer {
    double* data = nullptr;
    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
    }
    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
};

void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    int deviceCount = 0;
    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
    if (deviceCount == 0) {
        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
    }
    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1
        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
    if (workers == 1) {
        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
        return;
    }
    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
    threads.reserve(workers);
    try {
        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
                try {
                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
                } catch (...) {
                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
                }
            });
        }
    } catch (...) {
        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
        throw;
    }
    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
    for (const auto& error : errors) {
        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
    }
}

                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];

+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
+    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {
+        for (int device = 0; device < workers; ++device) {
+            // Partition whole tiles, including the final partial tile.
+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+            const size_t firstRow = tiles * device / workers * 64;
+            const size_t endRow = std::min(N, tiles * (device + 1) / workers * 64);
+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }

codex
Implemented CUDA in the two existing files. `matmul` now always uses a tiled, double-precision GPU kernel, with multiple GPUs used for large matrices. Executable name and CLI options are preserved.

Verified:
- CPU-reference hashes match through size 2049.
- Single- and four-GPU hashes match at size 8193.
- Compute Sanitizer reports no memory errors or races.
- Size 2049 improved from 24.2 seconds to 2.1 seconds.

No new source files or external dependencies were added.

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each block computes a 64 x 64 tile; each thread holds 16 outputs.
+// Cooperatively loaded tiles reuse each input across many output elements.
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int tile = 64;
+    constexpr int depth = 16;
+    __shared__ double aTile[tile][depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[depth][tile];
+    // Keep the original ascending-k accumulation in double precision.
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tile;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < tile * depth; offset += 256) {
+            const int ar = offset / depth;
+            const int ak = offset % depth;
+            const int bk = offset / tile;
+            const int bc = offset % tile;
+            aTile[ar][ak] = rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N
+                ? A[(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            bTile[bk][bc] = base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N
+                ? B[(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            double av[4], bv[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
+                av[t] = aTile[ty + t * 16][k];
+                bv[t] = bTile[k][tx + t * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += av[r] * bv[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + r * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + c * 16;
+            if (row < rows && col < N) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data), bytes));
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+void multiplyOnDevice(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+                      std::vector<double>& C, size_t N, int device,
+                      size_t firstRow, size_t rows) {
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    const size_t chunkBytes = rows * N * sizeof(double);
+    const size_t matrixBytes = N * N * sizeof(double);
+    DeviceBuffer a(chunkBytes), b(matrixBytes), c(chunkBytes);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(a.data, A.data() + firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(b.data, B.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    multiplyTiles<<<dim3((N + 63) / 64, (rows + 63) / 64), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        a.data, b.data, c.data, N, rows);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
+    // The synchronous copy also waits for the kernel and reports device errors.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(C.data() + firstRow * N, c.data, chunkBytes,
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a GPU");
+    }
+    // Smaller problems cannot amortize additional device setup and B copies.
+    const int workers = N < 8192 ? 1
+        : static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(deviceCount, N / 512));
+    if (workers == 1) {
+        multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, 0, 0, N);
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
+    std::vector<std::exception_ptr> errors(workers);
+    threads.reserve(workers);
+    try {

+            const size_t tiles = (N + 63) / 64;

+            threads.emplace_back([&, device, firstRow, endRow] {
+                try {
+                    multiplyOnDevice(A, B, C, N, device, firstRow, endRow - firstRow);
+                } catch (...) {
+                    errors[device] = std::current_exception();
+                }
+            });
         }
+    } catch (...) {
+        for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+        throw;
     }
+    for (auto& thread : threads) thread.join();
+    for (const auto& error : errors) {
+        if (error) std::rethrow_exception(error);
+    }
 }
